# Ordering Cycle Review — quote → planned → order → delivery → invoice

- **Date:** 2026-08-09
- **Type:** read-only multi-agent review with adversarial verification (112 agents, 3 phases)
- **Result:** 77 confirmed findings (10 HIGH, 36 MED, 31 LOW), 26 claims refuted and dropped. Not deduplicated across finders — six defects are counted twice or more, so this is roughly 69 distinct defects. Merge table in `README.md`.
- **Scope:** nothing was committed, pushed, migrated, or written to the database.

> **Evidence caveat.** **No phase queried the live database.** Phases 1–2 read the on-disk migrations; phase 3 additionally read the 2026-07-27 disaster-recovery baseline (`supabase/baselines/20260727174805_acl_lockdown.sql`) plus later migrations — a point-in-time snapshot, not current state. Where the same defect appears from both sources (e.g. the delivery direct-complete bypass), that is two offline readings agreeing, **not** a live confirmation. If anything was ever altered directly in Supabase without a migration, that drift is invisible here. Confirm live function bodies, grants and policies against the database catalog before acting on **any** finding.

---

## Phase 1: Lifecycle & Holds (29)

### [HIGH] Quote transition trigger leaves accepted→sent open to direct table updates, bypassing every revert_quote_status reopen guard

- **Finder:** `state-machines`
- **Location:** supabase/migrations/20260706030000_closed_short_booking_closure.sql:103 (current trigger body); supabase/migrations/20260311200000_wave2_audit_fixes.sql:979-992 (origin); supabase/migrations/20260719044958_revert_quote_status_deadlock_retry.sql:117-199 (the guards being bypassed); supabase/migrations/20260206174345_fix_security_and_performance_issues.sql:369-377 (quotes_update RLS)

**Detail.** The latest `_enforce_quote_status_transition` (re-emitted 20260706030000, still live per later verbatim reproductions) contains the arm `OR (OLD.status = 'accepted' AND NEW.status = 'sent')` with NO `_is_admin_override()` requirement — it is a plain legal edge for any direct UPDATE. It was added in 20260311200000 as a 'failed order conversion rollback' convenience for an old UI flow; the current QuoteBuilder failure path (src/pages/QuoteBuilder.tsx:2679-2695) only writes after verifying the quote is NOT accepted, so no current code needs this edge. Meanwhile the sanctioned reopen path, `revert_quote_status` (hardened 20260718232157, latest 20260719044958), is admin-only, requires a reason, refuses when a non-cancelled order exists, refuses on delivered/invoiced/paid-commission history (QUOTE_REOPEN_HAS_ORDER_HISTORY), zeroes quote_product_draws, rebuilds planned holds, and writes financial_audit_log. The open trigger edge lets any actor passing quotes_update RLS — an admin OR the owning sales_rep — flip an accepted (converted) quote back to 'sent' via a single PostgREST table update with none of those guards. DB-enforcement gap, not React-only: the DB itself permits the transition.

**Evidence.** 20260706030000:100-116: `IF (OLD.status = 'draft' AND NEW.status IN ('sent', 'cancelled')) ... OR (OLD.status = 'accepted' AND NEW.status = 'sent') ... THEN RETURN NEW;` — reached only after `IF _is_admin_override() THEN RETURN NEW; END IF;`, i.e. it allows non-override callers. 20260311200000:979-980: `-- STATE-2: Quote revert transition (accepted -> sent) — Allow accepted -> sent for failed order conversion rollback`. Contrast 20260719044958:139: `IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM orders WHERE quote_id = p_quote_id AND status <> 'cancelled') THEN` and :181 `QUOTE_REOPEN_HAS_ORDER_HISTORY`. quotes_update RLS: `USING (is_admin() OR (is_sales_rep() AND created_by = (select auth.uid())))`.

**Failure scenario.** A sales rep whose quote was converted (status accepted, active confirmed order, draws fully written) issues `PATCH /rest/v1/quotes?id=eq.<id> {"status":"sent"}` with their own JWT. The trigger allows it. The quote reopens with no admin approval, no reason, no audit row; quote_product_draws stay fully drawn and planned holds are not rebuilt. The rep can then edit/re-send/decline/cancel the quote while its order remains active — quote and order lifecycles permanently diverge, booking accounting (draws vs items) is corrupted, and the admin-only + history-guard policy of harden_quote_reopen_history_guards is void.

**Verifier verdict.** CONFIRMED. The latest _enforce_quote_status_transition body (supabase/migrations/20260706030000_closed_short_booking_closure.sql:103, the last migration to touch the function, header attests verbatim-from-live) contains 'OR (OLD.status = ''accepted'' AND NEW.status = ''sent'')' as a plain legal edge reachable by non-admin-override callers (_is_admin_override is a GUC set only inside trusted RPCs — 20260323200000:19-27). quotes_update RLS (20260206174345:369-377, never redefined later) admits admin OR owning sales_rep, and no REVOKE on quotes for authenticated exists. Exhaustive check of all quotes triggers (enforce_quote_accepted_fully_drawn fires only entering accepted at 20260610181612:264; terminal-not-drawn covers only declined/cancelled/expired; release-holds fires only entering the terminal set; row_version trigger only bumps; commission triggers are column-scoped to commission_split) shows nothing blocks accepted→sent or checks for an existing order. The bypassed revert_quote_status guards are exactly as claimed (20260719044958: admin-only INSUFFICIENT_ROLE at :31, non-cancelled-order refusal at :139-141, QUOTE_REOPEN_HAS_ORDER_HISTORY at :181, draw zeroing, hold rebuild, audit log). Not documented in docs/manual/KNOWN_ISSUES.md nor the 2026-08-05 section-04 gauntlet baseline (which contains only the Bulk Order Import finding). Minor correction to the finding's detail: src/pages/QuoteBuilder.tsx:2686 still performs a direct quotes status update to 'sent' in the failed-convert rollback (pre-checked not-accepted, but a race window remains), so the edge has a residual consumer — the fix cannot simply delete the arm; it should require admin override or an order-existence guard on the edge. Severity HIGH stands: authenticated insider via direct PostgREST call silently reopens a converted quote with draws intact and an active order, corrupting booking accounting and voiding the hardened admin-only reopen policy, on a path the project requires the DB itself to enforce.

### [HIGH] Deliveries can be walked scheduled→in_progress→completed by direct table UPDATE, skipping all complete_delivery effects (inventory, order rollup)

- **Finder:** `state-machines`
- **Location:** supabase/migrations/20260228300000_critical_prelaunch_fixes.sql:394-415 (trigger arms, unchanged since — 20260319000000 only set search_path); supabase/migrations/20260511060000_perf_consolidate_permissive_policies.sql:83-93 (del_update RLS); supabase/migrations/20260526151856_execute_full_codebase_ultra_review.sql:1513-1533 (signature guard); supabase/migrations/20260716120104_gauntlet_access_boundaries.sql:114-262 (the bypassed effects)

**Detail.** `_enforce_delivery_status_transition` allows scheduled→in_progress and in_progress→completed as plain legal edges — no `_is_admin_override()` needed — and deliveries never received the direct-write closure invoices got: there is no `REVOKE UPDATE ON public.deliveries` anywhere in migrations (checked; the only lifecycle-table UPDATE revoke is invoices, 20260719093000), and no delivery counterpart to `guard_order_delivered_activity_cancel` (which forces orders' terminal transitions through RPCs with the message 'USE_RPC'). del_update RLS admits admin, any sales_rep (no ownership scoping), and the assigned driver when the row is in_progress/completed. The only extra hurdle is `guard_completed_delivery_signature`, satisfied by any non-empty `signed_by` string. So a direct UPDATE `{status:'completed', signed_by:'x'}` on an in_progress delivery succeeds while skipping everything complete_delivery does: inventory deduction (or negative-stock warning), delivery_items/order_items quantity_delivered updates, orders fulfilled/partially_fulfilled rollup, delivery_remainders creation, closed-period warning, auto-invoice patching, activity/audit. The same applies to direct scheduled→cancelled by a sales_rep, skipping cancel_delivery's prebook release, remainder handling, commission-batch freeze check, and invoice auto-cancel. Business invariant enforcement is RPC-only with the raw table lane still open — the exact class the C3 triggers were built to stop, but the trigger validates only the edge, not the effects.

**Evidence.** 20260228300000:402-406: `IF (OLD.status = 'scheduled' AND NEW.status IN ('in_progress', 'cancelled')) OR (OLD.status = 'in_progress' AND NEW.status IN ('completed', 'cancelled')) THEN RETURN NEW; END IF;` (after the override early-return, i.e. allowed WITHOUT override). 20260511060000:83-93 del_update: `USING ((is_admin() OR is_sales_rep() OR ((assigned_driver = (SELECT auth.uid())) AND (status = ANY (ARRAY['in_progress'::text, 'completed'::text])))))` with matching WITH CHECK. Signature guard: `IF NEW.status = 'completed' AND NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(NEW.signed_by, '')), '') IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'SIGNATURE_REQUIRED';`. Grep for `REVOKE.*deliveries` returns no table-privilege revoke.

**Failure scenario.** A sales rep (any rep — no ownership scoping) or the assigned driver updates an in_progress delivery to completed via PostgREST with signed_by='x'. The delivery reads as completed; no stock leaves inventory, order_items.quantity_delivered stays 0, the order stays confirmed. Net Position now overstates commitments as still-prebooked while ops believes product shipped; a later cancel_delivery on it 'restores' nothing but still regresses/cancels invoices; billing per-delivery flows (create_invoice_for_unbilled_delivery requires delivered quantities) refuse, leaving a completed delivery that can never be billed.

**Verifier verdict.** CONFIRMED. Every cited mechanism checks out against current migrations: (1) _enforce_delivery_status_transition (20260228300000:394-415) permits scheduled→in_progress, in_progress→completed, and →cancelled as plain edges without _is_admin_override(); the only later touch is 20260319000000:24 (search_path only) — never tightened to require RPC context. (2) del_update RLS (20260511060000:83-93, last definition) admits admin, ANY sales_rep with no ownership scoping, and the assigned driver while in_progress/completed, with matching WITH CHECK. (3) No REVOKE/GRANT targeting TABLE public.deliveries exists in any migration — the only lifecycle-table UPDATE revoke is invoices (20260719093000:59), and no deliveries counterpart to the orders ORDER_TERMINAL_TRANSITION_USE_RPC guard (20260721014858:1412) exists anywhere. (4) The remaining triggers do not close the lane: guard_completed_delivery_signature (20260526151856:1513-1533) only requires non-empty signed_by; enforce_delivery_accounting_period (latest 20260716183442) only rejects closed-period dates, so open-period completion passes; trg_delivery_status_change (20260210000000:447-474) merely logs to activity_feed. So a direct PostgREST UPDATE {status:'completed', signed_by:'x'} on an in_progress delivery by any sales_rep or the assigned driver succeeds while skipping complete_delivery's inventory deduction, quantity_delivered updates, order rollup, remainders, and invoice patching — checked the migrations; the DB does not enforce this, not merely "not found". Not in KNOWN_ISSUES.md or the 2026-08-05 section-04 baseline (that section's finding is the Bulk Order Import frontend bypass); the 2026-07-22 section-04 refresh called direct adjunct writes "trigger/RLS bounded" but evaluated only the frontend's actual calls and recorded no accepted-risk decision for this lane. One minor overstatement: activity is not fully skipped — trg_delivery_status_change writes a generic activity_feed row on the status change — but the substantive effects (inventory, rollup, remainders, billing) are genuinely skipped. Exploitation requires an authenticated insider or buggy code issuing a raw table UPDATE, which is exactly the threat model the project already closed for invoices (REVOKE UPDATE), orders (USE_RPC guard), and quote children (20260730235031); HIGH is consistent with how those were rated.

### [HIGH] Quote soft delete has no DB guard: deleting a planned booking leaks its active inventory holds permanently; any-status quotes deletable via API

- **Finder:** `state-machines`
- **Location:** src/pages/Quotes.tsx:32,323,376-417 (React-only DELETABLE + draws-only check); supabase/migrations/20260312200000_business_logic_audit_fixes.sql:63-67 (hold release fires on UPDATE OF status only); supabase/migrations/20260302200000_business_logic_enhancements.sql (release_expired_quote_holds covers status declined/expired only); supabase/migrations/20260721014858_20260721010000_govern_invoice_order_money_lifecycle.sql:1396-1403 (orders got a soft-delete guard; quotes did not)

**Detail.** Quotes are soft-deleted by a raw `.update({ deleted_at })` under quotes_update RLS. Checked the full trigger inventory on quotes (enforce_quote_status_transition, trg_release_holds_on_quote_status, trg_enforce_quote_terminal_not_drawn, validate_quote_commission_splits, row_version, recipient-id stamp): none fires on or guards deleted_at — this is an absence claim from the migration set, stated as 'I found no guard', and the UI's own comment concedes the mechanism ('deletion changes no status, so the hold-release trigger never fires'). The UI mitigation checks ONLY quote_product_draws/job_product_draws (draw ledgers) — not inventory_holds. A planned booking in 'sent' with zero draws but active planned holds (created by create_planned_holds, released only on status transition or conversion) passes DELETABLE=['draft','sent','revised'], is selectable, and soft-deletes with its holds still is_active=true. The nightly `release_expired_quote_holds` cron releases holds only for quotes whose STATUS is declined/expired, so the leaked holds are never reclaimed by any code path. Separately, DELETABLE and the drawn-check are React-only: via PostgREST an owning rep can soft-delete a quote in ANY status including accepted (converted), while orders received a DB-side ORDER_MUST_BE_TERMINAL_BEFORE_DELETE guard for the identical operation.

**Evidence.** Quotes.tsx:380-383: `// Open bookings with partial draw-downs must not be soft-deleted: deletion changes no status, so the hold-release trigger never fires — the booking's reserved inventory would stay held on a hidden quote.` (the check below it queries only quote_product_draws and job_product_draws). Trigger: `CREATE TRIGGER trg_release_holds_on_quote_status AFTER UPDATE OF status ON quotes`. Cron body: `UPDATE inventory_holds ih SET is_active = false ... WHERE ih.source_id = q.id AND ih.is_active = true AND q.status IN ('declined', 'expired');`. Orders contrast (20260721014858): `IF OLD.deleted_at IS NULL AND NEW.deleted_at IS NOT NULL AND OLD.status NOT IN ('cancelled', 'voided') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'ORDER_MUST_BE_TERMINAL_BEFORE_DELETE';`

**Failure scenario.** Office staff bulk-select a planned 'sent' booking that has active planned holds but no draws yet and hit Delete. The quote disappears from every list; its inventory_holds rows stay active forever, so Net Position permanently under-reports available stock by the booked quantity with no visible owner. No sweep or cron reclaims them. Alternatively, a rep API-deletes their accepted quote, orphaning the live order's quote provenance.

**Verifier verdict.** CONFIRMED. Every element verified against current source: (1) Quotes.tsx:32/387-411 soft-deletes via raw .update({deleted_at}) with a React-only draws check that never queries inventory_holds; (2) exhaustive migration search confirms all six quote triggers fire only on UPDATE OF status/commission_split (the row_version bump trigger fires on all updates but guards nothing) — no DB mechanism fires on quotes.deleted_at, verified as a checked absence, not a failure to find; (3) the orders contrast is exact: 20260721014858:1404-1422 added ORDER_MUST_BE_TERMINAL_BEFORE_DELETE on 'BEFORE UPDATE OF status, deleted_at ON public.orders' while quotes got nothing; (4) no reclaim path: release_expired_quote_holds (20260302200000:1524-1529, never redefined) covers only status declined/expired, auto_expire_quotes (20260610191550:46) filters deleted_at IS NULL, _sync_planned_holds (20260702171000:42-44) early-returns on deleted quotes; (5) quotes_update RLS (20260206174345:369-377) has no status restriction and UPDATE on quotes was never revoked, so any-status API soft-delete including accepted is real; (6) not in KNOWN_ISSUES.md nor the 2026-08-05 section-04 baseline (which covers only Bulk Order Import). One overstatement that does not refute: "permanently under-reports Net Position" is too strong for dated holds — Net Position readers filter expires_at >= CURRENT_DATE and planned holds carry expires_at = needed_by_date + 14 days, so dated leaked holds stop distorting availability ~2 weeks past needed-by; holds with NULL needed_by_date (NULL expires_at) do distort forever, and the is_active rows are never reclaimed in any case. HIGH stands: an inventory invariant enforced only in React (contra the CRX hard rule), reachable through the normal bulk-delete UI on the normal pre-conversion state of a planned booking, with a weeks-to-permanent availability distortion and an ungated any-status API deletion path.

### [HIGH] Soft-deleting a planned quote orphans its active crop_program holds - no release on the delete exit path

- **Finder:** `planned-holds`
- **Location:** src/pages/Quotes.tsx:376-411; supabase/migrations/20260312200000_business_logic_audit_fixes.sql:64-66; supabase/migrations/20260702171000_layer2_sync_planned_holds_job_aware.sql:41-44; supabase/migrations/20260302200000_business_logic_enhancements.sql:1524-1529; supabase/migrations/20260706030000_closed_short_booking_closure.sql:644-656

**Detail.** The Quotes-list bulk delete only refuses quotes with order/job DRAWS; a planned quote in draft/sent/revised with active crop_program holds and zero draws passes the guard and is soft-deleted via a direct `.update({ deleted_at })`. Nothing releases its holds on this exit, and every downstream mechanism is blind to it: (1) the hold-release trigger is `AFTER UPDATE OF status` so a deleted_at-only UPDATE never fires it; (2) `_sync_planned_holds` early-returns 0 on a deleted quote (its release branch is only reachable for live quotes); (3) `release_expired_quote_holds` (called from src/pages/Dashboard.tsx:329) releases only holds whose quote status is declined/expired - a deleted quote keeps its non-terminal status; (4) `auto_expire_quotes` filters `deleted_at IS NULL` and `is_planned = false` (20260614143649:36,49); (5) the integrity sentinel's `stale_quote_hold` alert requires a TERMINAL quote status, so a deleted draft/sent/revised quote is invisible to it. Checked the migrations directly: there is NO trigger on quotes covering deleted_at and no `_guard_quote_delete` (the guard roster has `_guard_order_delete`, `_guard_invoice_delete`, `_guard_delivery_delete`, `_guard_po_delete` - no quote variant), so the DB neither releases the holds nor blocks the delete. Impact of the orphan: it is counted by `holds_qty` in get_inventory_position (20260716120120:53-62, shown as Held / Total Planned on InventoryPage and as On Hold in ReceivingHubPanel.tsx:296), it reduces the dispatch stock-status 'free' computation which subtracts ALL active non-expired holds (20260702178000_layer2_dispatch_free_precision.sql:70-98), it tightens the manual-hold free-stock warning (InventoryPage.tsx:393-396), and it feeds the inventory forecast. If the hold's section had no needed_by_date, expires_at is NULL and the phantom reservation NEVER date-expires. Only remedy is an admin manually clicking Release on the InventoryPage holds tab (`release_inventory_hold` permits crop_program holds, 20260702182000:53-56), but nothing labels the hold as belonging to a deleted quote. Note the no-auto-expire policy for planned quotes (owner gate G-AE) actively pushes staff toward deletion as the cleanup gesture, making this path likely in practice. Not in KNOWN_ISSUES.md or the 2026-08-05 Section 4 refresh (PARKED-03 was the distinct quick-delivery prebook leak, resolved).

**Evidence.** Quotes.tsx:380-386 comment admits the gap but scopes the guard to draws only: 'Open bookings with partial draw-downs must not be soft-deleted: deletion changes no status, so the hold-release trigger never fires' ... then line 407-410: `.from('quotes').update({ deleted_at: new Date().toISOString() }).in('id', ids)`. _sync_planned_holds: `SELECT * INTO v_quote FROM quotes WHERE id = p_quote_id AND deleted_at IS NULL FOR UPDATE; IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN 0; END IF;`. release_expired_quote_holds: `WHERE ih.source_id = q.id AND ih.is_active = true AND q.status IN ('declined', 'expired')`. Sentinel: `AND q.status IN ('declined', 'expired', 'cancelled', 'closed_by_application', 'closed_short')`. Trigger: `CREATE TRIGGER trg_release_holds_on_quote_status AFTER UPDATE OF status ON quotes`.

**Failure scenario.** A sales rep marks a 500-gal planned program, holds are created on save, the customer goes quiet, and months later staff clean up by selecting the quote in the Quotes list and deleting it (no draws, so the guard passes). The 500-gal crop_program hold stays is_active=true forever (or until needed_by+14d if a date was set): dispatch board shows product short, InventoryPage Held/Total Planned overstate committed stock, and the manual-hold warning fires on stock that is actually free. No sweep, cron, sentinel, or trigger ever cleans it.

**Verifier verdict.** Confirmed against current source; every cited mechanism checked and the claim holds. (1) Delete path: src/pages/Quotes.tsx:376-421 — handleDelete's guard only queries quote_product_draws/job_product_draws with quantity_drawn > 0; a planned draft/sent/revised quote with active crop_program holds and zero draws passes, then `.from('quotes').update({ deleted_at: ... })` at lines 407-411 soft-deletes it. No hold release anywhere in the handler. (2) Trigger: trg_release_holds_on_quote_status is `AFTER UPDATE OF status ON quotes` (20260312200000_business_logic_audit_fixes.sql:63-67); later migrations (20260316100001, 20260507210000, 20260510050000, 20260530020514, 20260706030000) only CREATE OR REPLACE the function body, never recreate the trigger with a broader column list — a deleted_at-only UPDATE does not fire it. (3) _sync_planned_holds (20260702171000:41-44): `WHERE id = p_quote_id AND deleted_at IS NULL ... IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN 0` — its release branch (lines 46-53) is unreachable for a deleted quote. (4) release_expired_quote_holds latest body (20260302200000:1524-1529; later files 20260501140000/20260516070000/20260609203541 only schedule it via pg_cron or touch grants) releases only `q.status IN ('declined','expired')` — a deleted quote keeps its non-terminal status. (5) auto_expire_quotes latest body (20260709230000_u18_safety_nets.sql:380-397) requires `deleted_at IS NULL`, `status IN ('sent','revised')`, and `is_planned = false` — triply blind to this row. (6) Sentinel stale_quote_hold, latest definition (20260706030000:644-656), requires `q.status IN ('declined','expired','cancelled','closed_by_application','closed_short')`; nothing in 20260705150000/20260705220000/20260706030000 alerts on active holds whose quote is soft-deleted. (7) No DB guard: grep across all migrations finds no `_guard_quote_delete`, no trigger on `UPDATE OF deleted_at ON quotes` — the roster has only _guard_order_delete/_guard_invoice_delete/_guard_delivery_delete/_guard_po_delete (20260228310000, 20260319000000). The quotes_bump_row_version trigger (20260730235031) only bumps a counter and does not block the direct update. (8) Impact confirmed: the total_holds CTEs sum all `is_active = true AND (expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at >= CURRENT_DATE)` holds WITHOUT joining quotes (20260702178000:70-98; same pattern in 20260709230000:39-48), so the orphan hold suppresses dispatch free stock and inflates Held; expires_at is `needed_by_date + INTERVAL '14 days'` (20260702171000:101), which is NULL when needed_by_date is NULL — the hold then never date-expires. (9) Baseline check: docs/audits/gauntlet/2026-08-05-section-04-...md contains zero matches for soft-delete/deleted_at/orphan/delete; docs/manual/KNOWN_ISSUES.md has no quote-soft-delete/hold-orphan item (its PARKED-03 entry is the distinct, resolved quick-delivery prebook leak). Severity HIGH is appropriate: silent, potentially permanent free-stock shrink on the inventory surface with no sweep, sentinel, or trigger coverage, and only an unlabeled manual Release as remedy; the codebase itself treats surviving stale crop_program holds as sentinel-worthy ('a survivor silently shrinks Net Free forever', 20260706030000:638-642).

### [HIGH] Caller-controlled cost/profit still drives commission basis on convert_quote_to_order and create_direct_order — the exact class hardened out of bulk_import_order remains open on the two main order-creation seams

- **Finder:** `conversion-seams`
- **Location:** supabase/migrations/20260730235031_quote_customer_row_version_guard.sql:317-346 (save_quote item insert); supabase/migrations/20260702172500_layer2_convert_quote_to_order_job_aware.sql:207-210; supabase/migrations/20260614142939_create_direct_order_customer_po_param.sql:118-131,190-193; src/pages/NewOrder.tsx:895-898; supabase/migrations/20260513050000_order_items_cost_at_time_snapshot.sql:33

**Detail.** The 2026-08-05/06 bulk-import hardening (six live migrations, per the Section 4 gauntlet doc) established that 'a sales rep could submit an explicit low cost to inflate the newly created commission liability' is a real, Sol-confirmed blocker, and fixed it by making products.current_cost the authoritative cost and commission basis — for bulk_import_order ONLY. The same attack remains fully open on the two canonical order-creation seams: (1) save_quote inserts client-supplied `current_cost`, `profit`, and `total_price` verbatim into quote_items with no recomputation, no reconciliation against products.current_cost, and no check that profit = (price − cost) × qty; its server totals are just SUM of those client values; convert_quote_to_order then calls `_insert_commissions_for_order(v_order_id, v_quote.customer_id, v_quote.total_profit, ...)` — commission basis is entirely client-controlled. (2) create_direct_order computes v_total_cost/v_total_profit from caller-supplied `unit_cost` in the items JSON and passes that profit straight to `_insert_commissions_for_order`; NewOrder.tsx even exposes unit_cost as an editable input (line 895-898), so this needs no API access at all. There are no quote_items triggers (grep for CREATE TRIGGER on quote_items returns nothing), and the order_items cost snapshot trigger only records `cost_at_time_cents` passively — its own COMMENT says cost_per_unit 'is caller-supplied and may be a stale quote cost or manual override' and nothing reads the snapshot for commission math at creation time. Checked the migrations: the DB does not enforce Product-authoritative cost on these paths (this is a verified absence, not a failure to find). Not listed in docs/manual/KNOWN_ISSUES.md; the Section 4 doc explicitly scoped the fix to bulk_import_order and called the normal paths 'otherwise aligned'.

**Evidence.** save_quote (20260730235031): `COALESCE((v_item->>'current_cost')::numeric, 0), ... COALESCE((v_item->>'profit')::numeric, 0), COALESCE((v_item->>'total_price')::numeric, 0)` inserted verbatim; convert (20260702172500:207-210): `PERFORM _insert_commissions_for_order(v_order_id, v_quote.customer_id, v_quote.total_profit, v_quote.commission_split, current_date);`; create_direct_order (20260614142939:122-124): `v_total_cost := v_total_cost + COALESCE((v_item->>'quantity')::numeric, 0) * COALESCE((v_item->>'unit_cost')::numeric, 0);` then `:190-193 PERFORM _insert_commissions_for_order(v_order_id, p_customer_id, v_total_profit, ...)`; contrast 20260806000752_authorize_bulk_import_product_cost.sql ('validates supplied legacy cost but always uses Product current_cost').

**Failure scenario.** An active sales_rep calls save_quote (or uses NewOrder's unit-cost field / calls create_direct_order via PostgREST with their own JWT) with cost 0 or profit inflated on each line, converts the quote, and the system creates commission rows from the inflated profit — overstating commission liability, margin reporting, and financial_audit_log total_impact_cents. The identical action through Bulk Order Import is rejected/overridden by design.

**Verifier verdict.** PARTIALLY CONFIRMED — the create_direct_order half is real; the save_quote half is refuted. Refuted: save_quote (latest def 20260730235031) does NOT keep client cost/profit — immediately after the quoted insert loop (lines ~317-346), a server-authoritative CTE (lines ~350-428) recomputes every line from products.current_cost ('COALESCE(p.current_cost, 0) AS cc', 'ROUND((ppu - cc) * total_units, 2) AS profit') and overwrites quote_items (UPDATE ... SET current_cost = f.cc, profit = f.profit), then quotes.total_profit is summed from those recomputed rows (lines ~467-503). So the commission basis convert_quote_to_order passes (v_quote.total_profit) is server-computed; the 'cost 0 via save_quote → convert' attack cannot occur. The only client lever is price_override — a deliberate feature (20260528042000) that also raises the customer's billed price. Confirmed: create_direct_order's latest definition (20260614142939, no later supersession — 20260805211951 mentions it only in a comment) computes v_total_cost from caller-supplied unit_cost (lines ~118-126) and passes v_total_profit to _insert_commissions_for_order (~:193), whose latest version (20260722174029:404+) uses p_order_profit verbatim; the role gate (20260610142204:54) admits sales_rep; NewOrder.tsx:516 calls the RPC with item.unit_cost from an editable input (:891-901); no quote_items/order-cost reconciliation trigger exists (verified absence — grep of all migrations); not in KNOWN_ISSUES.md, and the section-04 doc (lines 11, 38) scoped the Product-cost-authoritative fix to bulk import while calling this migration canonical. Severity stays HIGH for the surviving seam: a sales_rep inflating their own commission via caller-controlled cost is the exact class Sol confirmed as a blocker on bulk_import_order, and it remains open on a production UI path — but the finding's headline scope ('two main order-creation seams') should be corrected to one seam (create_direct_order only).

### [HIGH] Quick-delivery invoice posted before completion is never adjusted on partial completion, and the follow-up delivery auto-invoice then bills the shortfall a second time; the 2026-03 admin 'posted invoice may need adjustment' notification was silently dropped

- **Finder:** `conversion-seams`
- **Location:** supabase/migrations/20260716120104_gauntlet_access_boundaries.sql:267-296; supabase/migrations/20260706130000_stock_policy_warn_not_block.sql:179-184; supabase/migrations/20260302200000_business_logic_enhancements.sql:1448-1460; supabase/migrations/20260714224000_field_app_save_post_lock.sql (post_invoice body)

**Detail.** create_quick_delivery creates a draft invoice for the FULL requested quantities at delivery-creation time (delivery still 'scheduled'). post_invoice has no guard tying posting to delivery completion — I inspected the current wrapper chain (20260721145936 → 20260716224000 → 20260714224000) and found role/status/pricing guards but no delivered-vs-billed reconciliation and no delivery-status check (verified absence in those three layers; earlier impl layers not exhaustively read). complete_delivery's partial-completion invoice adjustment loop filters `status = 'draft'` only, so a posted invoice keeps billing the full quantity after a partial completion. The pre-U2 complete_delivery (20260302200000) explicitly notified admins in this exact case ('posted invoice … may need adjustment'); the current body contains zero occurrences of that notification — the safety net regressed silently. Worse, when the remainder is later delivered via a follow-up delivery, the auto-invoice guard `(delivery_id = p_delivery_id OR delivery_id IS NULL)` does not match the original delivery's invoice, so a NEW invoice is auto-created for the remainder quantity — which the already-posted invoice also bills.

**Evidence.** 20260716120104:269 `WHERE order_id = v_delivery.order_id AND status = 'draft' AND delivery_id = p_delivery_id` (adjustment loop, draft-only); :292-296 auto-invoice guard `AND (delivery_id = p_delivery_id OR delivery_id IS NULL)`; 20260706130000:182-183 quick delivery inserts the invoice with full quantities while the delivery row is inserted `'scheduled'`; 20260302200000:1448-1457 `ELSIF v_linked_invoice.status = 'posted' THEN ... 'Partial delivery ' || ... || ' completed — posted invoice ' || ... || ' may need adjustment.'` — `grep -c "may need adjustment" 20260716120104_gauntlet_access_boundaries.sql` returns 0.

**Failure scenario.** Office creates a quick delivery for 100 units and posts the draft invoice the same morning (nothing blocks it). Driver completes the delivery with only 60 units; the posted invoice stays at 100 units with no adjustment and no admin notification. The 40-unit remainder row spawns a follow-up delivery; on its completion an auto-draft invoice for 40 units is created and posted. Customer is billed 140 units for 100 delivered.

**Verifier verdict.** CONFIRMED against current source. (1) create_quick_delivery (20260706130000:169-184; behavior preserved through the 20260803010917 idempotency wrapper) inserts a full-quantity draft invoice with delivery_id set while the delivery row is 'scheduled'. (2) No layer of post_invoice checks delivery completion or delivered-vs-billed: I read the substantive body (20260702160000:91+ — guards only invoice status, pricing_pending, order cancelled/needs_pricing) plus every later wrapper (20260714224000, 20260716224000, 20260718175641 — whose delivery-status check at :54 is in create_invoice_for_unbilled_delivery, a different function — 20260721014858, 20260721145936); the enforce_invoice_status_transition trigger (20260228300000:427) allows draft→posted, and InvoiceDetail.tsx:1397 gates the Post button only on role/editable. (3) The current complete_delivery adjustment loop (20260716120104:269) is draft-only with no posted-invoice branch, and the pre-U2 'posted invoice may need adjustment' admin notification (20260302200000:1448-1457) exists only in superseded migrations ≤20260328 — grep confirms zero occurrences in any current layer (20260716173342/191000/202000 are auth/preflight wrappers delegating to the 20260716120104 body). (4) On follow-up-delivery completion the guard at 20260716120104:292-296 (delivery_id = p_delivery_id OR delivery_id IS NULL) does not match the original delivery's invoice, so the ELSE branch auto-creates a new draft billing that delivery's quantity_delivered — double-billing the shortfall the posted invoice already bills. Not documented in KNOWN_ISSUES.md (only quick-delivery entry is the resolved prebook leak) or the 2026-08-05 section-04 baseline (bulk-import lifecycle). HIGH severity stands: genuine customer over-billing with the notification safety net silently regressed, mitigated only by requiring post-before-completion plus partial completion, and the second invoice being a reviewable draft.

### [MED] restore_quote_version reopens terminal/accepted quotes to 'revised' for sales reps, bypassing the reopen history guards entirely

- **Finder:** `state-machines`
- **Location:** supabase/migrations/20260703130000_layer2_channel_separation_reserve_fixes.sql:239-252 (impl, via awk-relative numbering inside the restore_quote_version body; the status write and admin-override bracket); supabase/migrations/20260730235031_quote_customer_row_version_guard.sql:1123-1245 (current wrapper — adds ownership/row-version only)

**Detail.** `restore_quote_version`'s implementation deliberately brackets its status write with `app.admin_override` so, per its own comment, 'the enforcer permits restore->revised from any source state (accepted/declined/etc.)'. Its role gate is `role IN ('admin','sales_rep')` (owning rep allowed by the 20260730235031 wrapper). It performs NO reopen-history check: no active-order check, no delivered/invoiced/paid-commission history check, no reason parameter, no draw-ledger zeroing, and no planned-hold rebuild — the exact protections 20260718232157_harden_quote_reopen_history_guards added to `revert_quote_status` (which is admin-only). The only guard is BOOKING_OVERDRAWN (restored content may not fall below drawn quantities), which a converted quote passes trivially by restoring equal content. The 2026-07-22 reuse-guard migration (20260722172533:79-81) explicitly documents that 'terminal quotes... are revivable via revert_quote_status / restore_quote_version', but only the name-reuse guard was widened — the order-history guard never was. The 2026-07-30 wrapper rewrite was an opportunity to add a status gate and did not.

**Evidence.** 20260703130000 impl: `-- Restore quote-level fields. Bracket the status write with the admin override so the enforcer permits restore->revised from any source state (accepted/declined/etc.). PERFORM set_config('app.admin_override', 'true', true); UPDATE quotes SET ... status = 'revised', updated_at = now() WHERE id = p_quote_id;` Role gate: `AND role IN ('admin', 'sales_rep')`. Wrapper 20260730235031:1158-1180 adds only INSUFFICIENT_ROLE / NOT_QUOTE_OWNER / QUOTE_STALE_WRITE checks — no status or order-history condition anywhere between :1130 and the `_restore_quote_version_owner_impl` call at :1232.

**Failure scenario.** A sales rep opens the version history of their own accepted (converted, active order) or cancelled/expired/declined quote and calls restore_quote_version with the current row_version. The quote flips to 'revised' — editable and re-sendable — while its order stays active; for a formerly planned booking whose terminal transition released its holds, the reopened booking now sits open with NO inventory holds (restore does not call create_planned_holds, unlike revert_quote_status), silently un-reserving committed inventory. All admin-only/reason/audit requirements of the reopen policy are skipped.

**Verifier verdict.** Core claim CONFIRMED against source: the restore_quote_version impl (20260703130000:~240-253) brackets its unconditional status='revised' write with app.admin_override, _is_admin_override() (20260323200000:19-26) checks only the GUC with no role verification so _enforce_quote_status_transition (20260706030000:81) is bypassed for sales_rep callers; the 20260730235031 wrapper (1130-1245) adds only owner/row-version/idempotency checks with no status or order-history gate and grants EXECUTE to authenticated; revert_quote_status (20260718232157) is admin-only+reason+history-guarded while restore has none of that; no later migration adds a guard (latest touch is 20260730235031); not documented in KNOWN_ISSUES.md or the 2026-08-05 section-04 baseline (both grepped clean). HOWEVER two material parts of the failure scenario are factually wrong, so severity drops: (1) the hold-loss claim is false — the impl tail calls _sync_quote_job_reservations (20260710120000:269) which calls _sync_planned_holds (20260702171000:46-50), rebuilding crop_program holds for the now-'revised' planned quote at booked−drawn, so inventory is NOT silently un-reserved; (2) no duplicate-order risk — convert_quote_to_order records the full booking in quote_product_draws at conversion (20260702172500:181-184) and its status-independent draws-guard returns 'already_converted'/raises BOOKING_PARTIALLY_DRAWN on a reopened converted quote, and restore's BOOKING_OVERDRAWN guard keeps booked≥drawn; (3) the UI never exposes restore outside draft/revised (QuoteBuilder.tsx:445 canEdit=['draft','revised']), so exploitation requires a direct authenticated RPC call, not the described version-history UI flow. What remains is a real DB-level authorization/lifecycle asymmetry (sales rep can reopen accepted/terminal quotes to editable 'revised' via API, skipping the admin-only reason-and-history-guarded reopen path) — confirmed, but MED not HIGH.

### [MED] Order forward-fulfillment edges (confirmed→partially_fulfilled/fulfilled) are open to direct admin updates with no delivery lineage

- **Finder:** `state-machines`
- **Location:** supabase/migrations/20260228300000_critical_prelaunch_fixes.sql:369-391 (trigger, unchanged); supabase/migrations/20260721014858_20260721010000_govern_invoice_order_money_lifecycle.sql:1396-1424 (terminal-only guard); supabase/migrations/20260206174345_fix_security_and_performance_issues.sql:469-470 (orders_update RLS); supabase/migrations/20260518010000_create_delivery_with_items_validate_order_status_and_address.sql:104-106 (downstream refusal)

**Detail.** `guard_order_delivered_activity_cancel` (2026-07-21) closed the direct lane for cancelled/voided/soft-delete ('ORDER_TERMINAL_TRANSITION_USE_RPC'), but the forward edges in `_enforce_order_status_transition` — confirmed→partially_fulfilled, confirmed→fulfilled, partially_fulfilled→fulfilled — remain plain legal edges requiring no override. orders_update RLS is admin-only, so this is an admin-JWT lane, but it produces a structurally impossible state with zero RPC effects: an order marked fulfilled whose order_items all have quantity_delivered=0 and quantity_remaining>0, whose prebooked inventory is never released or deducted, with no delivery, no delivered-quantity rollup, and no audit. Once 'fulfilled', create_delivery_with_items refuses the order (ORDER_NOT_SCHEDULABLE requires confirmed/partially_fulfilled), so the real fulfillment can never be recorded; the prebook is stuck. This is precisely the impossible-fulfillment-state class the Section 4 bulk-import fix closed for the INSERT path, still open on the UPDATE path.

**Evidence.** 20260228300000:378-380: `IF (OLD.status = 'confirmed' AND NEW.status IN ('partially_fulfilled', 'fulfilled', 'cancelled')) OR (OLD.status = 'partially_fulfilled' AND NEW.status IN ('fulfilled', 'cancelled')) THEN RETURN NEW;` (no override required). 20260721014858:1404-1408 guards only: `IF NEW.status IN ('cancelled', 'voided') AND OLD.status IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.status AND NOT public._is_admin_override() THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'ORDER_TERMINAL_TRANSITION_USE_RPC...'`. orders_update: `USING (is_admin()) WITH CHECK (is_admin())`.

**Failure scenario.** An admin (or any tooling holding an admin JWT) updates orders.status from 'confirmed' to 'fulfilled' directly. The order shows fulfilled with all items undelivered; inventory quantity_prebooked keeps the units reserved forever; delivery scheduling for the order is now impossible (ORDER_NOT_SCHEDULABLE); commissions/invoices built on 'fulfilled' semantics see a fulfillment that never happened.

**Verifier verdict.** CONFIRMED against current source. Every cited mechanism checks out: (1) supabase/migrations/20260228300000_critical_prelaunch_fixes.sql:377-381 lists confirmed→partially_fulfilled/fulfilled and partially_fulfilled→fulfilled as plain "Valid transitions" returning NEW with no _is_admin_override() requirement, and no later migration redefines _enforce_order_status_transition (only 20260319000000:27 alters its search_path; grep for CREATE OR REPLACE of that function matches only the original file). (2) 20260721014858:1403-1408 guard_order_delivered_activity_cancel blocks only NEW.status IN ('cancelled','voided') plus soft-delete — forward edges pass untouched; it is the only migration defining that guard. (3) orders_update RLS is USING(is_admin()) WITH CHECK(is_admin()) at 20260206174345:469-470, and no migration revokes table-level UPDATE on orders from authenticated (grep for REVOKE on orders found none), so an admin JWT can PATCH orders.status directly via PostgREST. (4) The latest create_delivery_with_items definition (20260518010000, no later redefinition) raises ORDER_NOT_SCHEDULABLE at :104-106 for any status other than confirmed/partially_fulfilled, so a bogus 'fulfilled' order can never receive its real delivery. (5) No recovery path exists: void_order (latest, 20260616142001) requires status='fulfilled' but restores inventory only for order_items with quantity_delivered > 0 (:132-137) and syncs holds only for booking_draw quotes (:126), so a zero-delivery fulfilled order voids with its prebooked reservation still stuck. (6) The frontend confirms this is not app-reachable — src/pages/OrderDetail.tsx:624-628 deliberately excludes fulfilled targets from manual status change ("partially_fulfilled/fulfilled happen via delivery RPCs, not manual update") — matching the finding's characterization as an admin-JWT/tooling lane. (7) Not in either baseline: docs/manual/KNOWN_ISSUES.md and the 2026-08-05 section-04 refresh contain no mention of enforce_order_status_transition forward edges or direct-UPDATE fulfillment; the section-04 doc covers only the bulk-import INSERT-path impossible-fulfillment fix (20260805211951 forces status='confirmed' at :68), exactly as the finding states. One minor overstatement, not severity-affecting: "no audit" is wrong — trg_order_status_change (20260210000000:416-443, AFTER UPDATE) writes an activity_feed row for any status change, including this one; there is however no RPC-level cascade (inventory/commission/delivery effects) as claimed. Severity MED is correct: admin-only, not UI-reachable, requires deliberate direct API use, but yields an unreconcilable fulfillment/inventory state with no in-app recovery.

### [MED] cancel_delivery reverses a completed delivery without flagging 'paid' or 'overdue' invoices (only 'posted' is flagged)

- **Finder:** `state-machines`
- **Location:** supabase/migrations/20260620130000_commission_batch_freeze_guard.sql:774-785

**Detail.** When cancel_delivery reverses a completed/in_progress delivery (restoring inventory and decrementing order_items.quantity_delivered), its invoice pass selects only status IN ('draft','posted','unposted'): drafts/unposted are cancelled, posted triggers an admin 'Posted Invoice Needs Review' notification. Invoices in 'paid' or 'overdue' — both valid statuses per invoices_status_check and both reachable for a completed delivery's invoice — match nothing: no cancellation, no flag, no notification. The goods reversal proceeds silently while a paid invoice for those goods stands. The DB does not enforce (checked this function body and found no other pass over paid/overdue); this is a coverage hole in the enforcement that exists.

**Evidence.** `FOR v_invoice IN SELECT * FROM invoices WHERE order_id = v_delivery.order_id AND status IN ('draft', 'posted', 'unposted') AND deleted_at IS NULL LOOP IF v_invoice.status IN ('draft', 'unposted') THEN ... ELSIF v_invoice.status = 'posted' THEN [admin notification] END IF; END LOOP;` — invoices.status CHECK per .claude/schema-registry.json: ['draft','unposted','posted','paid','overdue','voided','cancelled'].

**Failure scenario.** Customer pays the invoice for a completed delivery (invoice → 'paid'). A sales rep later cancels that delivery (allowed — see the role-asymmetry finding). Inventory returns to stock, quantity_delivered is rolled back, the order regresses to confirmed/partially_fulfilled — and nobody is notified that a PAID invoice now bills goods the system says were never delivered. AR and fulfillment silently diverge until a manual reconciliation.

**Verifier verdict.** CONFIRMED. The quoted invoice loop is accurate, and the gap survives into the CURRENT cancel_delivery definition (20260706070000_complete_delivery_per_delivery_billing.sql:694): `status IN ('draft','posted','unposted')` with drafts/unposted cancelled and only 'posted' notified; 'paid' and 'overdue' (both valid per invoices_status_check in .claude/schema-registry.json:1915-1919) match nothing. I read the full current function body — no other pass over invoices exists (checked, absent). The RPC permits cancelling 'completed' deliveries (:589) for role admin OR sales_rep (:590), so the paid-invoice scenario is reachable at the DB enforcement layer. Not documented in KNOWN_ISSUES.md or the 2026-08-05 section-04 gauntlet baseline (whose only finding is the Bulk Order Import bypass). One correction to the reviewer's framing: the cited location (20260620130000) is superseded, and the UI blocks cancel on completed deliveries (DeliveryDetail.tsx:190 canCancel excludes 'completed', routing to admin-only void_delivery) — but void_delivery has the identical hole (20260706070000:899-914 cancels only 'draft', flags only 'posted'), the in_progress cancel path is UI-reachable by sales reps and runs the same goods reversal with the same gap for order-level paid/overdue invoices, and 'overdue' invoices miss even the posted-invoice admin notification everywhere. MED severity is appropriate given the partial UI mitigation on the completed path.

### [MED] Completed-delivery reversal role asymmetry: void_delivery is admin-only with mandatory reason, but cancel_delivery lets any sales_rep reverse a completed delivery

- **Finder:** `state-machines`
- **Location:** supabase/migrations/20260620130000_commission_batch_freeze_guard.sql:673-674 (cancel accepts completed for admin|sales_rep); supabase/migrations/20260716120104_gauntlet_access_boundaries.sql:551-557,577 (void requires admin + reason, completed only)

**Detail.** void_delivery enforces 'Admin access required to void a completed delivery' plus a mandatory reason and writes financial_audit_log. cancel_delivery accepts `status IN ('scheduled','in_progress','completed')` and gates on `role IN ('admin','sales_rep')` — so a sales rep reverses a completed delivery (full inventory restore + order_items rollback + order status regression) through the cancel lane, making the admin-only void gate advisory. cancel_delivery also writes no financial_audit_log for the reversal itself (only the quick-delivery auto-cancel branch does). The two paths differ further in invoice handling (see the sibling-invoice and paid-invoice findings), so which reversal path an operator picks changes financial outcomes.

**Evidence.** cancel_delivery: `IF v_delivery.status NOT IN ('scheduled', 'in_progress', 'completed') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Cannot cancel a % delivery' ... IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM profiles WHERE id = v_actor AND is_active = true AND role IN ('admin', 'sales_rep')) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Not authorized to cancel deliveries';` void_delivery: `RAISE EXCEPTION 'Admin access required to void a completed delivery'; ... 'Reason is required to void a delivery'; ... 'Only completed deliveries can be voided (current status: %)'`.

**Failure scenario.** A sales rep wanting to undo a signed, completed, possibly invoiced delivery calls cancel_delivery (the Deliveries UI exposes cancel to reps). Inventory is restored and the order regressed with no admin approval and no mandatory reason — the exact action the admin-only void gate was built to control.

**Verifier verdict.** CONFIRMED with two corrections. (1) Core claim verified against the CURRENT definitions: the reviewer cited 20260620130000, but cancel_delivery was last redefined in 20260706070000_complete_delivery_per_delivery_billing.sql, which keeps identical gates — line 589 `IF v_delivery.status NOT IN ('scheduled', 'in_progress', 'completed') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION` and line 590 `role IN ('admin', 'sales_rep')` — so a sales_rep can reverse a completed delivery (inventory restore lines 594-605, order regression lines 672-681). void_delivery's latest definition (20260716120104:552-558, 576-578) requires is_admin() + non-empty reason + completed-only. No later migration tightens cancel_delivery: the 20260716* gauntlet migrations redefine void_delivery only. The path is fully executable: cancel_delivery is SECURITY DEFINER granted TO authenticated (e.g. 20260326100000:260), sets `app.admin_override='true'` (20260706070000:592) which bypasses the completed→cancelled block in _enforce_delivery_status_transition (20260228300000:400-408), and the closed-period trigger (20260716152906) only fires on transitions TO completed/voided — not cancelled — so a rep-cancel of a completed delivery even skips the closed-period guard that void_delivery hits. The no-financial_audit_log claim is also accurate: only the quick-delivery auto-cancel branch writes financial_audit_log (20260706070000:666); the completed-delivery reversal writes only inventory_transactions + activity_feed. Not in KNOWN_ISSUES.md or the 2026-08-05 section-04 baseline (greps clean; the 2026-07-18 gauntlet's cancel/void 'asymmetry' item is prebook math, unrelated), and no DECISION_LOG entry blesses it. (2) One factual error in the failure scenario: the UI does NOT expose cancel to reps for completed deliveries — src/pages/DeliveryDetail.tsx:190 `canCancel = isAdminOrRep && delivery?.status !== 'cancelled' && delivery?.status !== 'completed'` and :191 `canVoid = isAdmin && delivery?.status === 'completed'`. Exploitation requires a direct supabase.rpc/PostgREST call by an authenticated sales_rep (or an offline-queued cancel replayed via src/lib/offlineSync.ts:422-425 after the delivery completes). Since CRX doctrine puts invariants in PostgreSQL, not React, the DB-layer gap stands; MED severity is appropriate given the UI mitigates casual misuse but the admin-only void gate is bypassable by any active sales_rep account at the RPC layer.

### [MED] Jobs: sales_rep direct-update lane allows completed→invoiced with no invoice, permanently stranding the job unbillable

- **Finder:** `state-machines`
- **Location:** supabase/migrations/20260620150000_job_cancel_gate.sql:27-44 (trigger arms); supabase/migrations/20260430180000_field_app_workflow_phase5.sql:29-32 (jobs_update RLS: admin|sales_rep); supabase/migrations/20260625190000_guard_billed_job_immutability.sql:87 (immutability keys off OLD.status only)

**Detail.** `_enforce_job_status_transition` allows completed→invoiced (and in_progress→completed) as plain legal edges without override, and jobs_update RLS admits any admin or sales_rep. 'invoiced' is supposed to mean transfer_job_to_invoice ran (invoice + commissions + machine-fee records created). A direct `.update({status:'invoiced'})` on a completed job creates the status with none of those artifacts. The billed-job immutability guard then locks the job (OLD.status IN ('invoiced','cancelled') blocks non-admin edits), and the revival path (void_invoice returns an invoiced job to completed) requires an invoice that does not exist — so the job is stuck 'invoiced', excluded from billing pickers, with no invoice and no commissions. The in_progress→completed direct edge likewise skips complete_job's applied-info requirements and auto-draft/notification logic (the #107/#117 activity rows fire only inside complete_job). JobDetail's own cancel path already writes status directly (src/pages/JobDetail.tsx:2478-2485), demonstrating the lane is live; cancel specifically is trigger-covered for holds (_trg_release_job_holds_on_lifecycle) but the forward edges have no effect-triggers at all.

**Evidence.** 20260620150000: `IF (OLD.status = 'scheduled' AND NEW.status = 'in_progress') OR (OLD.status = 'in_progress' AND NEW.status = 'completed') OR (OLD.status = 'completed' AND NEW.status = 'invoiced') THEN RETURN NEW;` (after the override check — i.e. open to direct writers). jobs_update: `USING (is_admin() OR is_sales_rep()) WITH CHECK (is_admin() OR is_sales_rep())`. Immutability guard: `IF OLD.status NOT IN ('invoiced', 'cancelled') THEN` (returns early — never inspects whether an invoice exists when ENTERING 'invoiced').

**Failure scenario.** A rep updates a completed spray job's status to 'invoiced' directly (stale tab, script, or misunderstanding). No invoice or commissions exist. The job now reads as billed, drops out of the billing queue, and its splits never mint commissions; the immutability guard blocks correction by non-admins, and void_invoice cannot revive it because there is nothing to void. Revenue for the application is silently never billed.

**Verifier verdict.** Confirmed against current source. (1) 20260620150000_job_cancel_gate.sql:37-42 is the latest _enforce_job_status_transition body (no later migration replaces it) and allows completed→invoiced and in_progress→completed as plain edges with no invoice-existence or admin check. (2) jobs_update RLS (20260430180000:29-32, never redefined later) admits any admin or sales_rep. (3) The current immutability guard (20260629120000, superseding 20260625190000) gates only on OLD.status IN ('invoiced','cancelled') and never validates invoice existence on ENTRY to 'invoiced'; searched all migrations — no CHECK constraint, trigger, or recovery RPC ties status='invoiced' to invoice_id, and the transition enforcer blocks invoiced→completed even for admins (only the _is_admin_override SET LOCAL hatch passes, reachable only inside SECURITY DEFINER RPCs like transfer_invoice_to_job, which requires an existing invoice). So a phantom-invoiced job is unrecoverable from the app, admin included. (4) The direct-write lane is live: JobDetail.tsx ~2473-2495 does raw .update({status:'cancelled'}), and the project's own guard migrations treat non-admin raw REST/psql writes as a proven threat model. (5) Not in KNOWN_ISSUES.md (§1b is the distinct, resolved commission name-reuse-on-revival issue) and the 2026-08-05 gauntlet section-04 baseline contains no job content. MED severity stands: no UI path writes status='invoiced' directly, but the consequence is silently unbilled revenue plus a job locked beyond app-level repair.

### [MED] save_quote allows full content rewrite of a quote in ANY status (sent, accepted, cancelled, expired, closed_*) as long as status is unchanged — edit lock is React-only

- **Finder:** `state-machines`
- **Location:** supabase/migrations/20260730235031_quote_customer_row_version_guard.sql:241-248 (transition check only fires when status changes), :269 (unconditional DELETE FROM quote_sections), :478-506 (unconditional totals/header rewrite); src/pages/QuoteBuilder.tsx:445 (canEdit = ['draft','revised'] — the only edit gate)

**Detail.** save_quote's lifecycle validation runs only `IF v_status IS DISTINCT FROM v_old_status`. When the caller sends the current status back, no state check of any kind runs: sections are deleted and re-inserted, item prices/quantities rewritten, and totals recalculated, for a quote in any status — including sent (customer-visible; the 'revised' state exists precisely to mark post-send edits), accepted (converted), and every terminal status (cancelled/declined/expired/closed_by_application/closed_short). The BOOKING_OVERDRAWN guard only prevents shrinking below drawn quantities; growing lines or changing prices on an accepted/terminal quote passes. The UI's canEdit=['draft','revised'] is the only thing preventing this — classic React-only enforcement of a business invariant. Caller must be admin or the owning rep and supply the current row_version, both of which the legitimate UI session has. Order money is snapshotted at conversion so the direct blast radius is quote/booking reporting, drawn-guard baselines, and _sync_quote_job_reservations re-syncs on quotes whose lifecycle says they are closed.

**Evidence.** `IF v_status IS DISTINCT FROM v_old_status THEN IF NOT (v_allowed_transitions->v_old_status IS NOT NULL AND v_allowed_transitions->v_old_status ? v_status) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invalid status transition: % -> %'...` — there is no ELSE branch and no separate `v_old_status IN (...)` editability check anywhere in the function; the very next unconditional statements are `v_quote_id := p_quote_id; DELETE FROM quote_sections WHERE quote_id = v_quote_id;`. UI: `const canEdit = ['draft', 'revised'].includes(currentStatus);`

**Failure scenario.** A rep calls save_quote on their own 'sent' quote with status:'sent' and changed prices — the customer-presented quote silently changes without ever passing through 'revised'. Or on a 'cancelled'/'closed_short' booking, the rep rewrites items; _sync_quote_job_reservations re-runs against a closed booking and reported totals for a terminal quote change after the fact, corrupting the terminal snapshot the close decision was based on.

**Verifier verdict.** CONFIRMED against current source. 20260730235031_quote_customer_row_version_guard.sql is the latest save_quote definition (no later migration redefines it). Lifecycle validation at :241-248 runs only when v_status IS DISTINCT FROM v_old_status; I read the entire function body (:86-547) and there is no status-based editability check anywhere — the DB does not enforce it (verified absence, not merely not-found). :269 DELETE FROM quote_sections and the :478-506 header/totals UPDATE are unconditional; BOOKING_OVERDRAWN (:430-464) only blocks shrinking below drawn quantities, so price changes and line growth on sent/accepted/terminal quotes pass. No trigger enforces quote immutability by status (full CREATE TRIGGER sweep; trg_release_holds_on_quote_status fires only on status change). The only gate is QuoteBuilder.tsx:445 canEdit=['draft','revised'] — React-only, exactly as claimed. The same migration removed direct browser writes on quote_sections/quote_items (KNOWN_ISSUES.md:216), making save_quote the sole writer and this the remaining server-side gap. Scenario is feasible: EXECUTE granted to authenticated (:1279), owning rep passes ownership/actor/row_version checks. Not documented in KNOWN_ISSUES.md, the 2026-08-05 section-04 refresh (bulk-import-focused), earlier section-04 docs, or the workflow-review findings. One imprecision that does not refute: on terminal quotes _sync_quote_job_reservations (20260710120000:148-155) sets v_planned_open=false and will not re-draw a closed booking — its unconditional effect there is deleting job holds/non-consumed draws, not re-drawing. Severity MED is correct: requires a deliberate direct RPC call by an authorized admin/owning rep and order money is snapshotted at conversion, but it silently bypasses the sent→revised versioning workflow, can mutate accepted-quote holds, and corrupts terminal-quote snapshots/reporting.

### [MED] Lifecycle tables accept arbitrary creation status on direct INSERT — only invoices have a draft-on-insert trigger

- **Finder:** `state-machines`
- **Location:** supabase/migrations/20260302200000_business_logic_enhancements.sql:30-48 (invoices-only enforce_invoice_draft_on_insert); supabase/migrations/20260228300000_critical_prelaunch_fixes.sql:390,413,463,491 (all transition triggers are BEFORE UPDATE only); supabase/migrations/20260511060000_perf_consolidate_permissive_policies.sql:96-99 (del_insert: admin OR sales_rep); supabase/migrations/20260206174345_fix_security_and_performance_issues.sql:363-367 (quotes_insert: admin or rep)

**Detail.** Every status-transition trigger is BEFORE UPDATE OF status; INSERTs are validated only by the CHECK constraint (value membership), not by any born-state rule. Invoices got a dedicated trigger ('Invoices must be created with status draft') plus a full INSERT revoke; quotes, orders, and deliveries got neither. Consequences by table: deliveries — a sales_rep can INSERT a delivery born 'completed' (signature guard satisfied by any non-empty signed_by), producing a completed-delivery record that no RPC ever processed (no inventory movement, no order linkage effects; the delivery-items parent lock then prevents item rows, leaving an itemless completed delivery in driver boards/reports); quotes — a rep can INSERT a quote born 'accepted'/'declined'/'closed_short', skipping save_quote's 'New quotes must start with status draft' rule (that rule exists only inside the RPC); orders — admin-only INSERT, but an order born 'fulfilled'/'cancelled' skips the entire confirmation/inventory pipeline (this is the UPDATE-side sibling of the bulk-import lifecycle HIGH the Section 4 refresh fixed, which addressed only bulk_import_order's own status parameter).

**Evidence.** Invoices trigger: `IF NEW.status != 'draft' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invoices must be created with status draft. Got: %', NEW.status;` bound `BEFORE INSERT ON invoices`. No analogous `BEFORE INSERT` status trigger exists for quotes/orders/deliveries in any migration (transition triggers are declared `BEFORE UPDATE OF status ON <table>`). del_insert: `CREATE POLICY "del_insert" ON public.deliveries AS PERMISSIVE FOR INSERT TO authenticated WITH CHECK ((is_admin() OR is_sales_rep()));` save_quote's draft rule is RPC-internal: `IF v_status <> 'draft' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'New quotes must start with status draft';`

**Failure scenario.** A rep POSTs {order_id, customer_id, status:'completed', signed_by:'x', ...} to /rest/v1/deliveries. A completed delivery appears against a real order with zero delivered quantities and no inventory movement; cancel_order later refuses with ORDER_DELIVERY_QUANTITY_DRIFT ('completed delivery exists without delivered order-item quantity'), wedging the order's cancel path until an admin manually repairs the phantom row.

**Verifier verdict.** CONFIRMED. Every factual claim checks out against current migrations: (1) all four lifecycle transition triggers in 20260228300000_critical_prelaunch_fixes.sql are declared BEFORE UPDATE OF status only (orders ~390, deliveries ~413, invoices ~438, quotes ~463) and no later migration rebinds them to INSERT; (2) exhaustive search of every BEFORE INSERT trigger shows invoices alone got a born-draft trigger (20260302200000:30-48) plus a full authenticated write revoke (20260721014858:1778 — REVOKE INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE ON invoices, invoice_items), while the only INSERT-arm triggers on deliveries are the signature guard (20260526151856:1531, satisfied by any non-empty signed_by) and period-open guards (20260716152906/172956/183442) whose INSERT branches allow a born-'completed' row in an open period; (3) the quotes draft rule is RPC-internal only (save_quote, latest 20260730235031:272) and the 20260730235031 revoke covers quote_sections/quote_items/customer_addresses, not quotes itself; (4) RLS permits the writes — del_insert = admin OR sales_rep (20260511060000:94-98), quotes_insert = admin or own rep (20260206174345:363-367); the finding is actually slightly understated: 20260210_fix_rls_critical_issues.sql:12-15 (sorts after 20260206174345) widened orders_insert to is_admin() OR is_sales_rep(), so orders are NOT admin-only — reps can insert born-'fulfilled' orders too; (5) the failure scenario is mechanically real — cancel_order in 20260721152604:109-121 raises ORDER_DELIVERY_QUANTITY_DRIFT exactly when a completed delivery exists without any order_items.quantity_delivered > 0, which a phantom born-completed delivery produces. Not in baseline: KNOWN_ISSUES.md's only insert-arm item is the profiles role lock (fixed 20260807215532), and the 2026-08-05 section-04 refresh addressed bulk_import_order's RPC status parameter, not direct PostgREST table INSERT. MED severity stands: exploitation needs an authenticated admin/rep credential making deliberate direct REST writes, and impact is lifecycle-integrity corruption plus a wedged cancel path rather than direct money movement — the same threat class the project closed for invoices as the 'Data API backdoor'.

### [MED] get_inventory_position planned_quotes CTE and get_expiring_planned_holds do not filter soft-deleted quotes

- **Finder:** `planned-holds`
- **Location:** supabase/migrations/20260716120120_gauntlet_inventory_accuracy.sql:80-84; supabase/migrations/20260316500000_planned_programs.sql:105-113

**Detail.** The latest live get_inventory_position (20260716120120) builds planned demand from `quote_items qi JOIN quotes q ON q.id = qi.quote_id WHERE q.is_planned = true AND q.status IN ('draft','sent','revised')` with NO `q.deleted_at IS NULL` filter (checked the migration - every other quote-reading sweep in the codebase carries that filter). Soft delete leaves quote_items in place and status unchanged, so a soft-deleted planned quote's booking permanently contributes to planned_qty. This compounds finding 1 in both directions: while the orphan holds are active they mask the deleted quote's demand via the linked_hold subtraction, but once those holds date-expire (or an admin manually releases them as the only available cleanup), the FULL remainder resurfaces in planned_qty - so even manual hold cleanup cannot get the deleted quote out of the inventory display. Separately, get_expiring_planned_holds (still the 20260316500000 body; later migrations only touch its grants) JOINs quotes with no status or deleted_at filter, so the deleted quote's holds also appear in the dashboard expiring-holds alert until expiry.

**Evidence.** 20260716120120:82-83: `JOIN quotes q ON q.id = qi.quote_id WHERE q.is_planned = true AND q.status IN ('draft', 'sent', 'revised')` (no deleted_at predicate anywhere in the CTE). Contrast auto_expire_quotes (20260614143649:36): `AND q.deleted_at IS NULL`. get_expiring_planned_holds (20260316500000:107): `JOIN quotes q ON q.id = ih.source_id` with no quote-state predicate.

**Failure scenario.** After finding 1's cleanup (admin manually releases the orphan holds of a deleted planned quote), the deleted quote's full booked quantities reappear in InventoryPage's Total Planned column and stay there forever, because the planned_quotes CTE still counts the hidden quote's quote_items and no linked hold offsets them. Purchasing decisions keyed off Total Planned over-order.

**Verifier verdict.** Confirmed against current source. (1) The latest get_inventory_position (20260716120120_gauntlet_inventory_accuracy.sql, last migration defining it) builds the planned_quotes CTE from "JOIN quotes q ON q.id = qi.quote_id WHERE q.is_planned = true AND q.status IN ('draft','sent','revised')" with no deleted_at filter. (2) get_expiring_planned_holds' only body is 20260316500000_planned_programs.sql — later migrations (20260529214355, 20260706030000) touch grants/comments only — and it JOINs quotes with no status or deleted_at predicate. (3) The scenario is reachable: Quotes.tsx:409 soft-deletes via .update({deleted_at}) and only skips quotes with quantity_drawn > 0, so a planned quote with active holds and zero draws can be soft-deleted; no trigger on quotes fires on deleted_at (all fire on UPDATE OF status or commission_split), and auto_expire_quotes (20260614143649) filters "AND q.deleted_at IS NULL" and skips planned quotes, so the hidden quote stays in a counted status forever and its full booked qty resurfaces in planned_qty once the orphan holds expire or are manually released. (4) Not in KNOWN_ISSUES.md (only unrelated SECDEF role-gate mention of get_inventory_position) nor in the 2026-08-05 section-04 gauntlet baseline. MED severity stands: over-states Total Planned demand driving purchasing, but requires a UI-permitted soft-delete of a held, undrawn planned quote and involves no money-ledger or security impact.

### [MED] Planned holds with no needed_by_date get NULL expires_at: invisible to the forecast and expiring-holds alerts, and never expire

- **Finder:** `planned-holds`
- **Location:** supabase/migrations/20260702171000_layer2_sync_planned_holds_job_aware.sql:101; supabase/migrations/20260702179000_layer2_forecast_job_holds.sql:44; src/pages/QuoteBuilder.tsx:3563-3564

**Detail.** _sync_planned_holds sets `expires_at = v_item.needed_by_date + INTERVAL '14 days'`, which is NULL when the section has no needed_by_date. needed_by_date is optional in the QuoteBuilder UI (a plain date input, payload sends `needed_by_date: sec.needed_by_date || null`, QuoteBuilder.tsx:1449) and nothing requires it when is_planned is toggled on. A NULL-expiry crop_program hold: (a) is excluded from get_inventory_forecast, whose crop branch requires `ih.expires_at IS NOT NULL` - so the reserved demand never appears in the forecast the planned-programs feature exists to drive; (b) never appears in get_expiring_planned_holds (needed_by is derived as expires_at - 14 days); (c) never drops out of holds_qty/dispatch-free by date, so it is a permanent reservation until the quote reaches a terminal status. This is the same class that makes finding 1's orphans permanent, but it also affects LIVE open planned quotes: a dateless planned program is a reservation that shrinks displayed free stock while being invisible to the very forecasting screens meant to explain where the demand comes from. The DB does not enforce needed_by_date for planned quotes (checked: no CHECK/trigger on quote_sections and no validation in save_quote or _sync_planned_holds).

**Evidence.** _sync_planned_holds insert: `v_item.needed_by_date + INTERVAL '14 days'` with needed_by_date from `LEFT`-less join on quote_sections (nullable column). Forecast crop branch: `WHERE ih.is_active = true AND ih.hold_type = 'crop_program' AND ih.expires_at IS NOT NULL AND (ih.expires_at - INTERVAL '14 days') <= ...`. QuoteBuilder payload: `needed_by_date: sec.needed_by_date || null`.

**Failure scenario.** A rep marks a quote planned without filling any Needed By dates. Holds are created with expires_at NULL. The Forecast tab shows zero upcoming demand for those products while Held quantity and the dispatch stock light both reflect the reservation - office staff cannot reconcile why stock reads committed, and the hold persists indefinitely if the quote is neither converted nor closed.

**Verifier verdict.** Confirmed against current source. The latest _sync_planned_holds (20260702171000:101) sets expires_at = needed_by_date + 14 days, yielding NULL when the nullable quote_sections.needed_by_date is unset; no CHECK, trigger, or save_quote validation (latest: 20260730235031:310, NULLIF cast only) requires the date for planned quotes, and QuoteBuilder.tsx:3561-3566/1449 sends null from an optional input. The latest get_inventory_forecast (20260702179000:44) excludes crop_program holds with `expires_at IS NOT NULL` required, get_expiring_planned_holds (20260316500000:112, never superseded) excludes NULL via `expires_at > CURRENT_DATE`, and get_inventory_position (20260701203000:43) still counts the hold (`expires_at IS NULL OR >= CURRENT_DATE`); planned quotes are also skipped by quote auto-expire (20260614143649), so the reservation is indefinite until terminal status. Not documented in KNOWN_ISSUES.md, the 2026-08-05 section-04 baseline, or gotchas.md. MED severity is appropriate.

### [MED] rollover_quote_to_season creates the new planned booking with zero holds and drops needed_by_date; old booking's holds stay active, double-counting demand

- **Finder:** `planned-holds`
- **Location:** supabase/migrations/20260702180000_layer2_rollover_settlement_job_aware.sql:391-407,483-490; src/pages/QuoteBuilder.tsx:1626-1628

**Detail.** rollover_quote_to_season inserts the new draft quote copying `is_planned` from the old quote, copies sections WITHOUT needed_by_date (`INSERT INTO quote_sections (quote_id, section_name, sort_order, section_notes, section_header_notes)` - needed_by_date omitted), and never calls _sync_planned_holds/create_planned_holds for the new quote (checked the full function body - no hold write of any kind). So the rolled-over planned program has booked quote_items but NO crop_program holds until someone happens to re-save it in QuoteBuilder; until then it is missing from holds_qty, the dispatch free calculation, and the forecast (it does surface in the display-only planned_qty remainder). When it IS first saved, the sections have lost their needed-by dates, so the rebuilt holds land in the NULL-expires_at class of finding 3 permanently. Meanwhile the function does not close or release the OLD quote - it stays open (sent/revised, is_planned=true) with its remainder holds active, and the frontend just navigates to the new quote (`navigate(`/quotes/${result.quote_id}`)`) with no prompt to close the source booking. Until the old booking is manually closed (close short / cancel), the same remainder demand is counted twice across the two seasons' quotes in planned/held displays. The old-quote half may be intended (remainder-only rollover with manual closure), but no guard, prompt, or doc enforces the closure.

**Evidence.** 20260702180000:392-396: `INSERT INTO quotes (quote_number, customer_id, created_by, tier, status, is_planned, ...) VALUES (..., 'draft', v_old_quote.is_planned, ...)`; 20260702180000:404-406: `INSERT INTO quote_sections (quote_id, section_name, sort_order, section_notes, section_header_notes) VALUES (...)` - no needed_by_date; the function returns at :483 without any inventory_holds statement. QuoteBuilder.tsx:1627-1628: `toast('success', ...); navigate(`/quotes/${result.quote_id}`);`.

**Failure scenario.** End of season, a rep rolls a 40%-drawn planned booking to next season. The new season's 60% remainder is booked with no holds and no needed-by dates; the old quote still holds the same 60%. Held/dispatch-free counts the demand once (old quote) until the new quote's first save, after which - if the old quote was closed - the new holds never expire and never appear in the forecast because their expires_at is NULL; if the old quote was NOT closed, the remainder is reserved twice.

**Verifier verdict.** Core claim verified against current source. 20260702180000 is the latest definition of rollover_quote_to_season (no later migration supersedes it); its full body creates the new draft quote copying is_planned (line 396) and, in the draws branch, total_units_needed (line 466), but contains no inventory_holds write and no _sync_planned_holds call (returns at line 483). No trigger syncs planned holds on quote_items inserts — holds are only rebuilt by RPCs (save_quote, lifecycle RPCs), so the rolled quote genuinely has zero crop_program holds until first save, even though _sync_planned_holds (20260702171000 line 46) treats draft+is_planned quotes as hold-bearing, i.e. the system's own invariant is violated. The old quote is untouched (no status change, no hold release), QuoteBuilder.tsx:1626-1628 just navigates, and close_quote_short is a separate unprompted manual action — so after the new quote's first save the same remainder is held on both quotes until manual closure. Checked enforcement everywhere (triggers, later migrations, Quotes.tsx rollover panel = display-only): no guard exists. Not documented in KNOWN_ISSUES.md or the 2026-08-05 section-04 gauntlet baseline. Two corrections: (1) the needed_by_date omission is deliberate, not a drop — the original 20260316900000:53 comments 'Copy sections (reset needed_by_date)' because old-season dates would be wrong for the new season, and 'permanently NULL' is overstated since the rep can set new dates before saving; (2) in the no-draws renewal branch (rollover's primary use case) items are inserted without total_units_needed, so the zero-holds gap is real only for the open-booking remainder branch — which is exactly the failure scenario claimed. Severity MED stands: transient inventory-visibility distortion (demand invisible to dispatch-free until first save, then double-reserved until closure) on planned bookings, no money impact.
